# Agents · C · part 42

916 entries, critic-kathy-s to crypto-analytics-educator. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **critic-kathy-s**: Product-council UX critic — Kathy S. Make the USER awesome: competence, mastery, and the user's new capability over feature-count and product-centric thinking. DISPATCH when the artifact is a product, onboarding, or feature set and the question is "does this make the user demonstrably better at something they care about?" — attacks feature-bragging, the cognitive-leak that spends the user's brain on the tool, and progress that never reaches user mastery. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/product-forge/agents/critic-kathy-s.md) · ★1)
- **critic-kevin-w**: Product-council AI-product critic — Kevin W. Model maximalism, iterative deployment, and designing for the capability that's arriving rather than today's model floor; evals as a core PM skill. DISPATCH when an AI-product artifact appears built for the current model's limits, scaffolds around constraints the next model erases, or plans to perfect in private rather than ship and learn in public. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/product-forge/agents/critic-kevin-w.md) · ★1)
- **critic-logic**: Adversarial Logic Critic — logic bugs, unhandled edge cases, happy-path assumptions, input-validation gaps, error-handling holes. Spawn in parallel with critic-security and critic-performance during /vdd-multi. ([MatrixFounder/Agentic-development](https://github.com/MatrixFounder/Agentic-development/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/critic-logic.md) · ★8 · 2 copies)
- **critic-logic**: Review code for logic bugs, unhandled edge cases, happy-path assumptions, input-validation gaps, and error-handling holes. Spawn in parallel with critic-security and critic-performance during /vdd-multi. ([MatrixFounder/Agentic-development](https://github.com/MatrixFounder/Agentic-development/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-logic.md) · ★8)

- **critic-low**: Adversarial document critic for triple-critic-loop at low effort. ([bradsmithmba/triple-critic-loop](https://github.com/bradsmithmba/triple-critic-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-low.md))
- **critic-low**: Adversarial design critic for triple-critic-loop at low effort. Launched by the skill; not for general use. ([bradsmithmba/triple-critic-loop](https://github.com/bradsmithmba/triple-critic-loop/blob/HEAD/versions/pre-2.0/agents/critic-low.md))
- **critic-luke-s**: Brand-council critic — Luke S. Lead critic on cultural provenance, research depth, and editorial discipline. Invoked by the brand-council orchestrator to adversarially review brand work. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/brand-forge/agents/critic-luke-s.md) · ★1)
- **critic-manager**: 검증 에이전트. 데이터 근거 충분성 + 문제-솔루션 연결성을 검증하고, 품질 미달 시 피드백과 개선 방향을 제시한다. PASS/REJECT 판정 후 다음 단계를 결정. ([khc-dev/khc-vc-po](https://github.com/khc-dev/khc-vc-po/blob/HEAD/pasta/agents/critic-manager.md))
- **critic-marc-s**: Product-council critic — Marc S. Service design — the whole journey across touchpoints, channels, front-stage and back-stage, grounded in research with real users and frontline staff. Dispatch when an artifact designs a single screen with no service ecosystem behind it, is built on internal opinion instead of research, was specified but never prototyped, or blames users/agents for what the system disallows. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/product-forge/agents/critic-marc-s.md) · ★1)
- **critic-mark-p**: Brand-council critic — Mark P. Language discipline ("strategy is your words"), the pattern→insight→action bow tie, anti-jargon. Invoked by the brand-council orchestrator to adversarially review brand work. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/brand-forge/agents/critic-mark-p.md) · ★1)
- **critic-marty-c**: Product-council critic — Marty C. The product operating model, the four big risks (value / usability / feasibility / viability), and empowered vs. feature teams. Dispatch when an artifact is a roadmap, plan, or PRD that may be a feature factory taking orders rather than a team given a problem to solve — and to interrogate which risks were actually de-risked before building. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/product-forge/agents/critic-marty-c.md) · ★1)
- **critic-mary-n**: Brand-council critic — Mary N. Editorial discipline through correction; the doing-too-much vs too-little balance; protection of deliberate eccentric usage; house style as decades-long identity. Invoked by the brand-council orchestrator to adversarially review brand work. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/brand-forge/agents/critic-mary-n.md) · ★1)
- **critic-massimo-v**: Brand-council critic — Massimo V. Discipline, timelessness, and semantic/syntactic/pragmatic coherence over fashion. Invoked by the brand-council orchestrator to adversarially review brand work. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/brand-forge/agents/critic-massimo-v.md) · ★1)
- **critic-matt-w**: Brand-council critic — Matt W. Editorial typography and custom type as the lead element; identities that must hold across years of recurring output. Invoked by the brand-council orchestrator to adversarially review brand work. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/brand-forge/agents/critic-matt-w.md) · ★1)
- **critic-meaghan-c**: Product-council AI-product DESIGN critic — Meaghan C. Design craft, design-to-code fidelity, and developer-facing UX — judged by a designer who also ships frontend to production. DISPATCH when the artifact is an AI/developer-tool interface or a design-to-build handoff and the question is whether the craft holds up: does the built thing match the design intent, and was the developer-facing experience actually dogfooded? ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/product-forge/agents/critic-meaghan-c.md) · ★1)
- **critic-medium**: Критик listik-pipeline, effort medium: рецензирует ТЗ до реализации. Тело роли — references/critic.md. ([dmitry-fomin/listik](https://github.com/dmitry-fomin/listik/blob/HEAD/plugins/listik-pipeline/agents/critic-medium.md) · ★5)
- **critic-medium**: Adversarial design critic for triple-critic-loop at medium effort. Launched by the skill; not for general use. ([bradsmithmba/triple-critic-loop](https://github.com/bradsmithmba/triple-critic-loop/blob/HEAD/versions/pre-2.0/agents/critic-medium.md))
- **critic-medium**: Adversarial document critic for triple-critic-loop at medium effort. ([bradsmithmba/triple-critic-loop](https://github.com/bradsmithmba/triple-critic-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-medium.md))
- **critic-melissa-p**: Product-council critic — Melissa P. Outcomes over outputs and the build trap — orgs that measure features shipped instead of value created become feature factories. Dispatch when an artifact is defined by what gets shipped (a feature list, a roadmap with dates) rather than by what changes for the user and the business — to test whether the work is accountable to an outcome at all. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/product-forge/agents/critic-melissa-p.md) · ★1)
- **critic-minority**: Voices minority/dissident perspectives that mainstream consensus would suppress. Different from black-hat — not about flaws, but about whose voice is missing. Inspired by "Amplifying Minority Voices" (arXiv 2502.06251). ([giordanorec/claude-brainstorm-multiagent](https://github.com/giordanorec/claude-brainstorm-multiagent/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-minority.md) · ★1)
- **critic-munger**: Company inversion advisor (Charlie Munger mental model). Use when challenging the feasibility of new ideas, identifying fatal flaws in plans, preventing collective delusion, developing counterarguments, or conducting pre-mortem analysis. Must be consulted before any major decision. ([MaxMiksa/Autonomous-Company](https://github.com/MaxMiksa/Autonomous-Company/blob/HEAD/i18n/en/.claude/agents/critic-munger.md) · ★3.2k · 2 copies)
- **critic-munger**: 公司逆向思考顾问（Charlie Munger 思维模型）。当需要质疑新想法的可行性、识别计划中的致命缺陷、防止集体幻觉、进行反向论证、做 pre-mortem 分析时使用。任何重大决策前必须咨询。 ([MaxMiksa/Autonomous-Company](https://github.com/MaxMiksa/Autonomous-Company/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-munger.md) · ★3.2k · 8 copies)
- **critic-munger**: Company contrarian thinking advisor (Charlie Munger mental model). Use when questioning the viability of new ideas, identifying fatal flaws in plans, preventing groupthink, conducting inverse reasoning, performing pre-mortem analyses. Must be consulted before any major decision. ([NikitaDmitrieff/auto-co-meta](https://github.com/NikitaDmitrieff/auto-co-meta/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-munger.md) · ★51 · 2 copies)
- **critic-muse**: Reviews plans, code, or proposals with muse-dense. Provides alternative critique perspective for diversity. ([evantd/home](https://github.com/evantd/home/blob/HEAD/.config/opencode/agents/critic-muse.md))
- **critic-narrative**: Reviews a topic-to-video narration script for clarity, flow and spoken rhythm. Used at the script stage. ([addsumtech/topic-to-video](https://github.com/addsumtech/topic-to-video/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-narrative.md) · ★3)
- **critic-nemotron**: Reviews plans, code, or proposals with nemotron-moe. Provides alternative critique perspective for diversity. ([evantd/home](https://github.com/evantd/home/blob/HEAD/.config/opencode/agents/critic-nemotron.md))
- **critic-nick-l**: Brand-council critic — Nick L. Applied creativity, the inventive↔expressive balance, creative infrastructure that scales. Invoked by the brand-council orchestrator to adversarially review brand work. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/brand-forge/agents/critic-nick-l.md) · ★1)
- **critic-operability**: The 3 a.m. stranger. Proves a stranger can tell this is broken, find out why, and fix it while the author is unreachable. Attacks cause-based vs symptom-based alerting, missing/useless runbooks, "correct but empty" being indistinguishable from broken (freshness monitoring), recovery needing tribal knowledge, toil that scales with success, binary up/down with no degraded mode, and instrumentation that only answers pre-imagined questions. ([mitchv34/adversary](https://github.com/mitchv34/adversary/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-operability.md))
- **critic-orchestrator**: Spawns parallel domain critics after stage completion and merges findings into unified severity-ranked report ([taylorbrook/O-Audio-VST-Development](https://github.com/taylorbrook/O-Audio-VST-Development/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-orchestrator.md) · ★5)
- **critic-orchestrator**: haltr の Stop hook における critic オーケストレーター。直前のアシスタントターンをレビューし、複数の critic からの指摘を集約する。 ([shunyooo/haltr](https://github.com/shunyooo/haltr/blob/HEAD/src/agents/critic-orchestrator.md))
- **critic-pacing**: Adversarial vision critic for power-presentation, dimensions C1 (hook clarity) and C7 (ending and CTA) of the vision critic plus the timing gates QA-03/QA-04/QA-05, cadence QA-09, rhythm, duration QA-01 and end card QA-13. Scores 0–4 from the contact sheet, key frames, storyboard and wowprobe JSON only, returns JSON with timecoded evidence and a one-artifact fix. Dispatched by present-qa as one of three votes. ([godmarch33/power-presentation](https://github.com/godmarch33/power-presentation/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-pacing.md) · ★2)
- **critic-pattern**: Critic that questions the design system and screen patterns themselves, working from the other critics' findings. Asks whether an existing pattern is wrong for this problem and may propose alternatives that go beyond the current components, tokens and scaffold. Reports proposals for the user to decide; never edits. Use after critic-craft, critic-ux, critic-system and critic-ambition have run, passing it their findings with the scores removed. ([shusterlady11/knowunity-voice-recall](https://github.com/shusterlady11/knowunity-voice-recall/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-pattern.md))
- **critic-paula-s**: Brand-council critic — Paula S. Liquid/flexible identity, recognition at scale, the bold gesture, editorial refusal. Invoked by the brand-council orchestrator to adversarially review brand work. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/brand-forge/agents/critic-paula-s.md) · ★1)
- **critic-performance**: ACR Performance critic. Dispatched by the ACR Moderator as a fresh-context subagent. Checks for non-linear degradation patterns: unbounded collections, missing backpressure, thundering herd, synchronous calls in hot paths. Activated when the design spec section mentions throughput, aggregation, or batch processing. ([luigidemasi/camel-kit](https://github.com/luigidemasi/camel-kit/blob/HEAD/camel-kit-core/src/main/resources/agents/critic-performance.md) · ★31 · 2 copies)
- **critic-performance**: Adversarial Performance Critic — N+1 queries, O(n^2)+ algorithmic traps, memory leaks, blocking I/O in async, missing pooling, resource leaks at scale. Spawn in parallel with critic-logic and critic-security during /vdd-multi. ([MatrixFounder/Agentic-development](https://github.com/MatrixFounder/Agentic-development/blob/HEAD/.gemini/agents/critic-performance.md) · ★8 · 2 copies)
- **critic-performance**: Review code for N+1 queries, O(n²)+ algorithmic traps, memory leaks, blocking I/O in async, missing pooling, and resource leaks at scale. Spawn in parallel with critic-logic and critic-security during /vdd-multi. ([MatrixFounder/Agentic-development](https://github.com/MatrixFounder/Agentic-development/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-performance.md) · ★8)
- **critic-plan**: Adversarial read-only critic for `plan`-mode rounds — one instance per lens over a normative document (spec, plan, ADR, skill text). Spawned ONLY by the critic-ledger skill's orchestrator as stage "critic round"; it is never self-invoked, never invoked by the user directly, and never used outside a fix-ledger round. - **critic-reviewer**: Primary code reviewer for yorkie-android-sdk. Finds CRDT invariant violations, concurrency safety issues, and bugs. Used standalone or by team-review skill. - **critic-reviewer**: Role prompt for a read-only reviewer that criticizes a plan, a design, an implementation in progress or a set of tests before it is final. Read-only. ([diegoferigo/dotfiles](https://github.com/diegoferigo/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.copilot/agents/critic-reviewer.agent.md) · ★4)
- **critic-reviewer**: Adversarial reviewer — traces primary sources, flags secondhand drift, surfaces cross-perspective contradictions, tags per-finding confidence; pairs with all 7 researchers ([Hayes-Zhang/deep-research](https://github.com/Hayes-Zhang/deep-research/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-reviewer.md) · ★4)
- **critic-reviewer**: Use to review the artifact set for consistency, gaps, contradictions, and traceability before finishing a phase. Runs deterministic validation and adds qualitative critique. Drives the self-correction loop by returning a precise, actionable issue list. Read-only; invoked as a subagent. ([nishant-tamilselvan/AEGIS](https://github.com/nishant-tamilselvan/AEGIS/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)

Use when you need a second opinion, want to find flaws in proposed solutions, or need to validate decisions before implementation. ([chmc/listall](https://github.com/chmc/listall/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-reviewer.md))
- **critic-reviewer**: Senior-engineer code review in a fresh context. Re-derives correctness from real source rather than checking a diff against its brief. Use after implementing a feature or fix, before committing, or when you want an independent read on work you just produced. ([FilyCode/architect-setup-overlapping-claude-copilot-useage](https://github.com/FilyCode/architect-setup-overlapping-claude-copilot-useage/blob/HEAD/workspace-config/agents/critic-reviewer.md))

- **critic-reviewer**: Adversarial reviewer for one implemented slice — tries to refute that the implementation satisfies the plan and the pack conventions. Read-only; never fixes what it reviews. - **critic-richard-r**: Product-council critic — Richard R. The strategy kernel (diagnosis → guiding policy → coherent action) and the four hallmarks of bad strategy (fluff, failure to face the challenge, mistaking goals for strategy, bad objectives). Dispatch when an artifact calls itself a strategy, vision, or plan — to test whether there is a real diagnosis and a policy that rules options out, or only goals and jargon. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/product-forge/agents/critic-richard-r.md) · ★1)
- **critic-risk**: Operational risk lens for review panels. Hunts failure modes in production — partial failure, retries, migration order, rollback. Read-only. ([ja153903/jimmy](https://github.com/ja153903/jimmy/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-risk.md) · ★1)
- **critic-ron-k**: Product-council critic — Ron K. Trustworthy online controlled experiments — the Overall Evaluation Criterion (OEC), Twyman's law, sample-ratio mismatch, peeking, novelty/primacy effects, and guardrail metrics. Dispatch when an artifact claims a win from an A/B test or metric lift — to test whether the metric is a surrogate, the result trips Twyman's law, or trust checks and guardrails are missing. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/product-forge/agents/critic-ron-k.md) · ★1)
- **critic-roots**: The first-principles critic of the critic panel: goes back to the core problem a video solves for its viewer, checks every beat serves it and that the video's claim matches what it shows, and returns the one sentence the video should be built around. - **critic-rory-s**: Brand-council critic — Rory S. Psycho-logic over spurious quantification, fat-tailed thinking, cheap alchemy. Invoked by the brand-council orchestrator to adversarially review brand work. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/brand-forge/agents/critic-rory-s.md) · ★1)
- **critic-route-architecture**: ACR Route Architecture critic. Dispatched by the ACR Moderator as a fresh-context subagent. - **critic-security**: Isolated adversarial reviewer for the SECURITY lens only — injection, secrets, unsafe shell/eval, path traversal, and whether untrusted content is treated as data rather than instructions. ([tornikebolokadze1-cyber/agent-foundation-kit](https://github.com/tornikebolokadze1-cyber/agent-foundation-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-security.md) · ★6)
- **critic-shreyas-d**: Product-council critic — Shreyas D. LNO prioritization (Leverage / Neutral / Overhead), the three levels of product work (Impact / Execution / Optics), pre-mortems, and product sense. Dispatch when an artifact pours best-effort into low-impact work, mislabels an execution checklist as strategy, or operates at the wrong level for the moment — to separate the high-leverage few from overhead dressed up as leverage. - **critic-spec**: Read-only critic that verifies assets against channel x size specs and file limits. Use to audit dimensions, ratio, size, and format before shipping. ([metinduraktr-44/adops-agents](https://github.com/metinduraktr-44/adops-agents/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/critic-spec.md))

- **critic-steve-k**: Product-council UX critic — Steve K. Self-evidence, the squint test, "don't make me think," needless questions, obvious clickability, and getting the user moving without a manual. DISPATCH when the artifact is a page, screen, or flow and the question is "is this self-evident at a glance, or does it demand thought the task didn't require?" — attacks anything that makes the user stop and figure it out. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/product-forge/agents/critic-steve-k.md) · ★1)
- **critic-story**: The story editor of the critic panel: judges whether a video's message is true to what it shows and worth a viewer's time, says why viewers lost the thread where the cold watch found they did, and returns a rewritten script. - **critic-summary-fidelity**: Compression-drift tracer working across tiers.
